    14	## Definition
    15	Surfaces enabling AI coding agents (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor) and developers to read and write the same ontology vault as humans: MCP servers, terminal CLI, in-app connect flow, and the ACP executor layer that directly launches coding agents already installed by the user.

    20	  full/read-only initialize inventory from active registry

    26	  ACP executor detection and launching with isolated settings within the app
    27	  (`capabilities/acp-runtime`)
    28	- Excluded: the graph schema itself (that belongs to the graph-modeling domain)
    29	
    30	## Project Meaning Handover
    31	
    32	`finalize_project_meaning` bundles the competency answer for human-readable project Markdown into body digest and graph/source provenance after graph write, vault validation, and project compile.
    33	the sidecar does not store raw answers, raw witnesses, or private source root/remote.
    34	Subsequently, the new MCP process's `agent_brief.meaningAssessment` re-validates against the current Markdown and inventory; if source currentness cannot be confirmed, it closes as `review_required` even if a stored receipt exists.
    35	
    36	The CLI `agent-brief --project <slug>` is not an option to merge results from multiple projects, but a selector that explicitly specifies one project containment tree to read the same MCP `agent_brief`.

    13	## Definition
    14	
    15	A single location (`/agents/`) on this computer where you **receive, install, attach, fix, and initiate conversations with** AI coding tools. It is the eighth destination of the rail.
    16	
    17	## Why Destination (2026-08-20, Chapter 90)
    18	
    19	Until 2026-08-20, this task occupied two cells in the settings sheet ("Executor"·"MCP Connection"), while a separate connection sheet on the map handled the same function. **Three addresses for one task**.
    20	
    21	The rationale is not screen width (measured install app: 46–47% of the sheet remains empty on the normal path). The rationale is **container**:
    22	
    23	- Modals block the background and own Esc. You cannot see the map while receiving 52MB.
    24	- **Settings are where values are chosen**, and this is an **operational task with progress**.
    25	
    26	This mirrors the separation of skills from documentation (2026-08-09): different questions asked imply different destinations.
    27	
    28	## What This Section Contains
    29	
    30	1. **Executor List**: Tools confirmed on this device are expanded first; the rest are collapsed.
    31	2. **Connection Check**: Review eight steps and fix what can be fixed right here.
    32	3. **App-Specific Install**: Download Node and CLI only within the app folder. Version pinning · hash
    33	   verification · command source preview (Chapters 88·89). Progress and completion remain on screen, persisting even if the window is closed and reopened.
    34	4. **MCP Connection**: Configuration that exposes this folder to external agents. Also available via web.
    35	   MCP attaches to the **folder**, not the screen (2026-08-01 Chapter).
    36	
    37	## Boundaries
    38	
    39	- **API Keys and workspaces remain in settings.** The former is governed by the 2026-08-16 "Path Freeze · Non-highlight", while the latter involves a different axis of Vault's response (`local-vault-management`).
    40	- **Draw the MCP configuration panel only after opening Vault** (owner confirmation 2026-08-21). Without Vault, there is no configuration to save; saying "cannot save" at that point refers to a file that does not yet exist.
    41	- **Eight rails are the upper limit** (owner signature). In the minimum window (720px), the eighth rail leaves only 8px remaining and enters; on zoom level 1.1 with width >2400, it requires 761px, exceeding limits, so rail scrolling is enforced alongside the cap.
    42	- Programs cannot be launched on the web. Explain the reason, where to go, and **what can still be done on this screen**.
    43	
    44	## Rationale
    45	
    46	- src/views/agents/ui/AgentsPage.tsx: Main destination body
    47	- src/widgets/app-settings-menu/ui/AcpRuntimeSettings.tsx: Executor list · check
    48	- src/widgets/app-settings-menu/ui/AgentSetupSection.tsx: MCP connection section (Vault gate)
    49	- src/features/acp-doctor/ui/AgentDoctor.tsx: Eight checks · repairs · install progress

   264	pub(crate) type LoginProbe<'a> = dyn Fn(&str, &Path, &[&str], &str) -> Option<bool> + 'a;
   265	
   266	/// How to look at the filesystem — injected so tests can judge without a real disk.
   267	/// If a check asks "what is on this machine" in a machine-dependent way, that
   268	/// check only goes green on the developer's machine.
   269	pub(crate) struct FsProbe<'a> {
   270	    /// Is there something executable in that location?
   271	    pub is_executable: &'a dyn Fn(&Path) -> bool,
   272	    /// The names directly under a directory (empty list if absent).
   273	    pub list_dir: &'a dyn Fn(&Path) -> Vec<String>,
   274	    /// Read a small text file (returns None if missing). Used only to read
   275	    /// the single file where nvm writes its "default version".
   276	    pub read_text: &'a dyn Fn(&Path) -> Option<String>,
   277	    /// Asks that CLI "are you logged in". `None` = did not ask (unknown).
   278	    ///
   279	    /// In production this briefly launches the CLI and looks at **the exit code only**.
   280	    /// Tests plug in a fake — we do not let a test spawn a real process just for this one verdict.
   281	    pub login_ok: &'a LoginProbe<'a>,
   282	}
   283	
   284	/**
   285	 * The commands that ask "are you logged in" — **only the ones we have actually measured.**
   286	 *
   287	 * ## Why this is needed (owner's remark, 2026-08-16)
   288	 *
   289	 * *"I already had claude code and codex installed, but I still had to press the
   290	 * button on each agent to set it up? Atlas shows 'ready' immediately — this needs verification"*
   291	 *
   292	 * The remark was correct. Our "ready" only checked **whether the file was in place**. But
   293	 * we were saying that even to someone who had installed but not logged in, and when that
   294	 * person opens a conversation it dies with `Authentication required` (a failure we had already measured).
   295	 *
   296	 * ⚠️ **We do not read the output. Exit code only.** `claude auth status` returns the email
   297	 * and even the organization ID (measured). We have no reason to read that, and reading it
   298	 * becomes the kind of thing the trust charter forbids — even without showing it on screen,
   299	 * we do not let it enter process memory at all.
   300	 *
   301	 * Measured values (2026-08-16): `claude auth status` 300ms · `codex login status` 45ms,
   302	 * both exit 0 while logged in.
   303	 */
   304	pub(crate) const LOGIN_PROBE: &[(&str, &[&str])] = &[
   305	    ("claude-acp", &["auth", "status"]),
   306	    ("codex-acp", &["login", "status"]),
   307	];
   308	
   309	fn login_probe_args(runtime_id: &str) -> Option<&'static [&'static str]> {
   310	    LOGIN_PROBE
   311	        .iter()
   312	        .find(|(id, _)| *id == runtime_id)
   313	        .map(|(_, args)| *args)
   314	}
   315	
   316	/// The `bin` directories of Nodes installed by nvm — **the version the user chose to use comes first**.
   317	///
   318	/// nvm keeps a separate directory per version and the shell puts only one of them on PATH.
   319	/// The app does not go through that shell, so it must pick directly — and **picking the newest is the wrong answer.**
   320	///
   321	/// Measurement on this machine (2026-08-16) showed exactly that: `claude` remained only in
   322	/// the bin of `v22.15.0` while the user's real `claude` is `~/.local/bin`. Version
   323	/// directories are **where stale copies of globally installed CLIs pile up**, so rummaging
   324	/// through any version picks a binary the user's shell would never use.
   325	///
   326	/// Hence the order: ① the version `~/.nvm/alias/default` points at ② the rest in numeric
   327	/// descending order. And this list is appended at the **very end** of the candidates (`candidate_bin_dirs`).
   328	fn nvm_bin_dirs(home: &Path, probe: &FsProbe<'_>) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
   329	    let root = home.join(".nvm");
   330	    let versions = root.join("versions").join("node");
  1345	pub(crate) fn prepare_runtime_isolation(
  1346	    runtime_id: &str,
  1347	    app_data_dir: &Path,
  1348	    home: Option<&Path>,
  1349	    cli: Option<&Path>,
  1350	    path_env: &str,
  1351	) -> Result<(&'static str, PathBuf), String> {
  1352	    // Two separate questions, asked in the order that matters. Eligibility first: a runtime whose
  1353	    // gate has not been measured must not reach a conversation even if the app *could* isolate its
  1354	    // config. Conflating the two is how decision (111) happened.
  1355	    if !chat_eligible(runtime_id) {
  1356	        return Err(format!("permission-gate-unsupported:{runtime_id}"));
  1357	    }
  1358	    let env = config_env_for(runtime_id)
  1359	        .ok_or_else(|| format!("permission-gate-unsupported:{runtime_id}"))?;
  1360	    let dir = prepare_isolated_config(runtime_id, app_data_dir, home, cli, path_env)
  1361	        .map_err(|reason| format!("isolation-failed:{reason}"))?;
  1362	    Ok((env, dir))
  1363	}

codex
현재 구현 기준으로, Ontology Atlas의 ACP runtime capability는 **“사용자의 코딩 에이전트를 앱 안에서 ACP v1로 실행하고, 현재 볼트의 MCP와 안전한 쓰기 관문을 붙이는 기능”**입니다. 온톨로지 스키마나 MCP 자체를 ACP가 소유하는 것은 아닙니다. [ACP capability 정의](/Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/docs/ontology/capabilities/acp-runtime.md:17)

먼저 문서 드리프트가 하나 있습니다. 온톨로지 문서에는 아직 “현재 Claude만”이라고 적혀 있지만, 최신 구현과 2026-08-24 결정에서는 Codex도 검증을 통과해 in-app chat 대상에 들어갔습니다. 따라서 아래는 최신 코드·결정 원장 기준입니다. [CHAT_ELIGIBLE]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/src-tauri/src/acp.rs:170) · [Codex 복귀 결정]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/docs/DECISIONS.md:20201)

| 경계 | 포함하는 것 | 제외하는 것 | 그렇게 한 이유 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 실행기 | 설치 상태 탐지, ACP 프로세스 실행, 앱 전용 설정 격리, 세션 종료 | 등록된 모든 에이전트를 곧바로 앱 채팅 대상으로 취급하지 않음 | 앱이 실제로 권한 관문을 검증한 실행기만 안전하다고 말할 수 있음. 현재는 `claude-acp`, `codex-acp`만 해당 |
| Atlas 연결 | `session/new`에 현재 볼트의 `atlas-vault` MCP 서버를 주입하고, 같은 온톨로지 도구를 사용 | ACP가 MCP를 대체하거나 별도의 온톨로지 API를 만듦 | Atlas의 고유 자산은 ACP가 아니라 로컬 Markdown 볼트와 MCP 도구이기 때문입니다. ACP는 그 위에 놓이는 실행 경로입니다. [vault MCP 연결]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/src/features/acp-session/model/vault-mcp-server.ts:3) |
| 읽기/쓰기 | Atlas 읽기 도구는 볼트 안에서 자동 진행. 예: `get_concept`, `find_path`, `query_ontology`, `validate_vault`, `index_project` 등 | Atlas 쓰기 도구는 모두 변경 검토로 보냄. 새 도구가 읽기 목록에 없으면 자동으로 write 취급 | 의미가 바뀌는 작업은 사람의 판단과 provenance가 필요하기 때문입니다. [읽기 목록과 fail-closed 정책]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/src/features/acp-session/model/atlas-tool-policy.ts:1) |
| 쓰기 승인 | `allow_once` 또는 `reject_once`, typed ontology change review, Codex의 경우 서버 측 MCP consent | semantic write의 `allow_always`, 무조건 자동 승인, 승인 불능 클라이언트에서의 쓰기 | Codex `read-only`가 직접 파일은 막아도 MCP 쓰기는 통과시킨 실측이 있었으므로, 쓰기를 수행하는 MCP 서버 자체에 관문을 둡니다. 관문을 표시할 수 없으면 fail-closed로 거절합니다. [MCP write consent]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/mcp/src/write-consent.mjs:1) |
| 파일 범위 | 세션의 작업 폴더는 검증된 볼트. 볼트 내부는 자동 허용될 수 있고, 외부·미확인 경로는 질문 | `/`, 홈 디렉터리 자체, OS/app bundle, 외부 경로의 자동 쓰기, 세션 중 바뀐 symlink 경계 | 잘못 선택한 폴더에서 에이전트가 시스템 파일을 건드리는 사고를 막고, 경로 문자열이 아니라 정규화된 실제 경로를 기준으로 판단하기 위해서입니다. [경로 판정]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/src-tauri/src/acp.rs:1408) |
| 작업 모드 | 안전성이 측정된 모드와, 아직 검증되지 않았음을 표시한 모드 | `bypasspermissions`, `agent-full-access`, Codex의 `agent` 등 권한 관문을 제거한다고 측정된 모드 | 모르는 모드를 안전하다고 간주하지 않기 위해서입니다. 단, 새롭지만 실제로 안전할 수도 있는 모드를 숨기는 것도 부정확하므로 “unverified”로 노출합니다. [mode safety]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/src/features/acp-session/model/mode-safety.ts:19) |
| 프로토콜 범위 | ACP v1의 세션 초기화·prompt·update·permission request·세션 수명 관리 | ACP client가 file read/write, terminal, elicitation capability를 직접 선언하는 것 | ACP capability 선언만으로 실제 CLI의 도구 권한이 통제되지 않는다는 실측이 있었기 때문입니다. 실제 관문은 런타임 설정과 MCP 서버에 둡니다. [ACP client 경계]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/src/features/acp-session/model/acp-client.ts:7) |
| 데이터·제품 경계 | 로컬 stdio MCP, 사용자가 이미 보유한 Claude/Codex 구독 인증, 앱 전용 설정 디렉터리 | Atlas API key, Atlas backend/login, Atlas가 provider 통신을 기록한다고 주장하는 것, 웹에서의 프로세스 실행 | 볼트는 로컬 Markdown/Git의 source of truth이고, 선택한 에이전트가 자신의 provider로 보내는 prompt·context·tool result는 별도 provider 경계입니다. [신뢰 경계]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/docs/guide/trust.md:5) |

소유권도 분리되어 있습니다.

- `agent-integration`은 실행기 탐지·실행·MCP 연결을 담당합니다.
- MCP capability는 실제 온톨로지 읽기/쓰기 도구를 담당합니다.
- `graph-modeling`은 스키마와 typed ontology change review를 담당합니다.
- ACP는 이들을 새로 복제하지 않고 연결합니다. [도메인 포함/제외]( /Users/stark/orca/workspaces/oh-my-ontology/main-2/docs/ontology/domains/agent-integration.md:23)

즉, 이 capability의 핵심 경계는 **“에이전트를 앱 안으로 들이는 것”은 포함하지만, “에이전트에게 Atlas와 컴퓨터 전체에 대한 무제한 권한을 주는 것”은 제외한다**는 데 있습니다.
